# Fact-check report

Manuscript: `paper/manuscript.md`. Generated 2026-10-26 by factcheck.py. Inputs: bibliography `paper/bibliography.json`; Screening rows with DOI 127; text files 116; extraction studies 32; PRISMA counts 18.

Text check: FOUND = the number occurs in the cited source near the sentence's key terms (a FOUND is still only a string match: read the quote); WEAK = the number occurs but none of the key terms are nearby; NOT FOUND = the number does not occur in the source text (the claim is unsupported, or the number was rounded or derived: quote the source or mark NOT SUPPORTED); NO TEXT = no .txt for that source; NO CITATION = numeric claim without a key in its sentence or paragraph; COUNTS = an uncited flow count that matches prisma_counts.md. CSV: MATCH / MISMATCH against the extraction CSVs for the cited study, CHECK = equals a different column than the nearest term suggests. PRISMA: MATCH / MISMATCH against prisma_counts.md. Fill the Verdict column (OK / FIX: ... / NOT SUPPORTED) when verifying.

## Verification summary (step 10, 2026-10-26)

Verdicts were written by nine Claude Sonnet 5.5 verifier subagents (medium effort), one batch of 12 to 15 items each, from the article texts only, then checked and carried across to this regenerated report by the step-10 agent (Claude Fable 5.1), who also read three PDF pages directly (Sumi 2003 Table 3; Labreche 2018 Table 1; La Grow 2007 Table 1). The Verdict column holds the verifier's line (`VERDICT | page | quote | note`) or a `JUSTIFIED` line for items with no source text to check. AI verification is assistance: the reviewer reads every NOT SUPPORTED, DERIVED and JUSTIFIED line.

**Script summary before the fixes** (step 9 manuscript, no Wu 2024 text): 342 claims; FOUND 253, COUNTS 4, WEAK 6, NOT FOUND 12, NO TEXT 36, NO CITATION 31; CSV MISMATCH 30; PRISMA MISMATCH 14; exit 2.

**After the fixes** (this report; Wu 2024 mapped to the Europe PMC rebuilt text): 344 claims; FOUND 263, COUNTS 4, WEAK 8, NOT FOUND 12, NO TEXT 24, NO CITATION 33; CSV MISMATCH 31; PRISMA MISMATCH 14; exit 2.

**Verdicts over the 186 items in the "To verify" list:** SUPPORTED 87, DERIVED 24, JUSTIFIED 61, NOT SUPPORTED 13 (all resolved or labels, see below), WRONG SOURCE 1 (script label only).

**Manuscript changes made in this step** (all in `paper/manuscript.md`, then re-rendered):

1. Sumi 2003 domain correlations (-0.61 walking, -0.63 going out, -0.64 total) confirmed on the PDF page; both `[VERIFY]` tags removed. The text layer prints the minus sign as U+2AFA, which is why the script reports NOT FOUND.
2. "the questionnaire tracked [field] at least as well as acuity" was wrong for Sumi 2003 (acuity r = 0.74 versus field r = -0.64): sentence rewritten to say so.
3. Wu 2026 PPV 0.93 re-attributed to "one-item low vision screening" (p7), not to Q97.
4. Activity Inventory "up to 459 tasks" and "hundreds of tasks" removed (not in Massof 2007); minutes stay labelled unverified.
5. Table 3: Gresset 2002 reference-positive counts (12, 4) marked as back-calculated; Labreche 2018 Q3 specificity changed from 0.86 (0.77-0.92) to 0.82 (0.72-0.89) with a footnote, because the source prints "65/79 86% (83-72)" and the fraction and percentage disagree. The same value was then applied to `analysis/extraction/dta.csv` as an advisory resolution (`resolutions.csv`) and the synthesis table and Figure 2 regenerated, so the package agrees; the reviewer confirms the cell.
6. Wu 2026 added as a second citation for the 20-degree legal-blindness criterion in the Box.
7. Citation marker moved after the full stop in the PPP sentence.

**Still open, with justification:**

- Script NOT FOUND 12: Sumi 2003 (5 items; Unicode minus, confirmed on the PDF), the -0.64 that the script attributes to Tabrett (it belongs to Sumi, the third key in the sentence), and the Wu 2026 reference-positive counts 264 and 73 plus one Wilson bound (DERIVED from Table 1 p3: 385 - 121; 50 + 23).
- Script NO TEXT 24: HOW WE SEARCHED counts (equal to `paper/prisma_counts.md`), the 20% second-reviewer sample (`paper/methods_draft.md`), QUADAS-2 domain counts, GQL-15 items and minutes (Nelson 2003 not in the corpus), the Box referral rule (flagged [VERIFY]), the clinical vignette, the two unretrieved papers.
- Script NO CITATION 33: figure, table, step and list numbers, byline and author-block placeholders, model version numbers, the reviewer-override counts (Counts tab: 15 changed of 40).
- CSV MISMATCH 31 and PRISMA MISMATCH 14: nearest-term artefacts (a CI bound compared with `n`, PubMed 78 compared with the 166 total); every one was read by a verifier and the source supports the manuscript value.
- `[VERIFY]` tags remaining in the manuscript (4) plus `[UPDATE]` (1) and `[untested]` (1): Ernst 2025 referral proportion (ID 130, no text), AAO PPP referral criteria (ID 135, no text), the Box field threshold and PPP alignment, the reviewer-override count and the "counts of corrected values" line in the AI disclosure, the Box timing.
- Catalogue values labelled "(unverified)" in Tables 1 and 2 (minutes for NEI VFQ-25, Activity Inventory, IVI, GQL-15, GAL-9; Activity Inventory task count).
- The two index questions are quoted as printed in Wu 2026 Table 2 and the Discussion ("can you usually read the posted menus?"); the abstract omits "usually", and the plan doc's wording follows the abstract.

## Second draft (2026-10-28)

The manuscript was redrafted on 28 October 2026 (Claude Fable 5.1, no subagents). The per-item verdicts above belong to the 26 October text; sentences that were not changed keep them. The scripted check of the second draft is in `paper/factcheck_report_draft2.md` / `.json` (379 claims: FOUND 274, COUNTS 4, WEAK 7, NOT FOUND 11, NO TEXT 38, NO CITATION 45; exit 0). The 11 NOT FOUND and 7 WEAK items are the same Sumi 2003 Unicode-minus values, the derived Wu 2026 reference-positive counts (264, 73) and Box list numbers already justified above. New claims and their status:

- Ernst 2025 (ID 130, no text): 522 patients, 88% qualifying as low vision, 14 referred (2.7%), referral associated with acuity not field. Source: the PubMed abstract in the Screening export. Tagged `[VERIFY: abstract values; full text not retrieved]`.
- Kaleem 2017 (ID 134, no text): following the PPP went with 2.5 times the odds of being a high referrer; 22% followed it. Abstract only; tagged.
- Potter 2024 (ID 23, no text): NHATS self-reported visual difficulty, sensitivity 0.16, specificity 0.96, 4,999 adults (abstract reports 15.8 and 96.3). Abstract only; tagged. Not in Table 3 because the report was not retrieved.
- Stalin 2025 (ID 30, no text): 112 inpatients, reduced habitual vision 48.7%, three questions sensitivity 0.74. Abstract only; tagged. Not in Table 3.
- Wittenborn ACS question via Wu 2026 p1-2: sensitivity 0.70, specificity 0.88 (legal blindness), sensitivity 0.43 (low vision), 438 patients. FOUND in the Wu 2026 text; the primary paper is not in the bibliography and is cited through Wu 2026.
- Rowe 2025 (ID 38) CVSQ "reading problems": sensitivity 0.84, specificity 0.75, n = 461 (217 positive), Table 4.1 p16; CIs approximate (†). Matches `analysis/synthesis/accuracy_table.md`; new reference 28.
- PPV near 0.34 at 10% prevalence: our arithmetic from sensitivity 0.89 and specificity 0.81 (0.089 / (0.089 + 0.171) = 0.34); labelled "(our arithmetic)".
- Kappa 0.41 (95% CI, 0.10-0.71) for reviewer vs first-pass AI: `analysis/screening/ta_reviewer_vs_ai1*` via `paper/methods_draft.md`.
- Minimal search edit 137 records and eight-arm revision 593 records: `paper/search_strategy_note.md`; 36 backward-citation candidates: plan doc step 3 status.
